Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. But registry says the command has `--gpu 0`. 

```text
Ah, I see the issue now. The framework uses CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES to map logical device indices to physical GPUs, so `--gpu 0` refers to the first device in that list, not necessarily physical cuda:0. The worker's nvidia-smi output shows the actual physical GPU assignment, which could be cuda:1 even though the script specifies `--gpu 0`.

For scheduling purposes, what matters is whether I have a free GPU slot. With `gpu_ids: [cuda:0, cuda:1]` and one experiment already running, there should be one GPU available for the next job.

I need to move these four experiments from PREPARING to PENDING and request Director approval, following the project's workflow. Looking at the sprint timeline, the trace length ablation is plan_006, and I should understand what approval criteria the Director needs before proceeding.

The pragmatic approach: one GPU is occupied by the running experiment, so the other should be free. I'll submit the ablation experiments for approval and start one immediately once approved, then queue the rest as resources become available.
```

```text
1	---
2	name: on-experiment-pending
3	description: 实验进入 PENDING 状态时的审批工作流——发送飞书审批卡片，根据批准/拒绝更新状态。当实验状态变为 PENDING 时使用。
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	- workflow
7	- approval
8	- cron-hook
9	tools:
10	- read_registry
11	- update_registry
12	- feishu_send_approval_card
13	- move_idea
14	version: 1
15	roles:
16	- agent
17	---
18	
19	# 实验审批工作流
20	
21	## 触发方式
22	
23	由 cron hook 自动触发。实验进入 PENDING 状态时（高风险变动，如多文件改动、大幅参数变化）触发。
24	
25	## 步骤（严格按顺序）
26	
27	### Step 1: 了解待审批实验
28	
29	- `read_registry(exp_id)` — 获取实验信息（hypothesis、risk_description、变更范围）
30	
31	### Step 2: 发送飞书审批卡片
32	
33	- `feishu_send_approval_card(exp_id, hypothesis, risk_description)` — 发送卡片
34	  - 卡片内容：实验假设 + 变更范围 + 风险说明 + 两个按钮（批准/拒绝）
35	  - 用户点击按钮后，飞书 webhook 会触发后续处理
36	
37	### Step 3: 汇报等待状态
38	
39	- 回复：已发送审批卡片，等待用户批准
40	- 说明：批准后自动进入提交队列；拒绝后实验退回 ideas.md ACTIVE 分区
41	
42	## 批准路径（由飞书 webhook 回调触发，非本 skill 处理）
43	
44	- `update_registry(exp_id, "status", "QUEUED")` — 进入队列
45	- 继续 submit-experiment 工作流
46	
47	## 拒绝路径（由飞书 webhook 回调触发，非本 skill 处理）
48	
49	- `update_registry(exp_id, "status", "DONE")` + `update_registry(exp_id, "conclusion", "killed")`
50	- `update_registry(exp_id, "notes", "人工审批拒绝：{拒绝原因}")`
51	- `move_idea(from_section="IN_PROGRESS（进行中）", to_section="ACTIVE（待验证）", index=N)` — 退回待验证
52	
53	## 工具调用预算
54	
55	最多 **3 次**（1× read_registry + 1× feishu_send_approval_card + 1× 状态更新）
56	
57	## 禁止事项
58	
59	- **不要**自动批准实验——审批必须等人工操作
60	- **不要**轮询等待飞书回调——发完卡片立即返回
61	
```

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50
```
